# The Blackwood family — GM dossier

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-BLACKWOOD-DOSSIER"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0001"></div>


## GM only

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0002"></div>


*To the mining village, the Blackwoods are a wealthy new house: generous, protective and always first to help. In truth, they are a tiefling crime family, and they've come to Silverwood to take everything.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0003"></div>


### What They Want

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0004"></div>


**The mythril.** The Blackwoods want control of Silverwood's mines, roads and trade, without ever being seen to take them. Their method is always the same: **create a crisis, then step in as the rescuer.**

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0005"></div>


### What They Don't Know

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0006"></div>


**Something in the Hells is using them.**

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0007"></div>


For centuries, Lord Xanthos has foiled the plans of fiends, devils and invaders from other planes. A power in the Hells is tired of it. It can't reach him directly, so it is using mortal pawns to weaken his town, cut it off and bring him down.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0008"></div>


- **Belial**, the head of the family, thinks he's the one in charge. He isn't.
- **Vashkar**, the family's butler, is the infernal handler planted in the house, quietly steering them.
- **Azazel** made his own bargain for his armour. He knows Hell is involved, and he thinks it's working for *him*.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0009"></div>


{{note
##### Keep it in the background
The players fight the Blackwoods, not Hell. The infernal patron explains the rakshasa, Azazel's armour and the fiends at the tower, and leaves a hook for a sequel. It never needs to be named.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 33}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Blackwood family — GM dossier

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-BLACKWOOD-DOSSIER"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0010"></div>


### The Family

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0011"></div>


| Name | Who they are | In the campaign |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **Belial** | Father. Head of the family and a criminal mastermind. | **Offstage.** Gives orders from afar. |
| **Naamah** | Mother. Despises traitors. She would rather have a dead child than a disloyal one. | **Offstage.** |
| **Azazel** | Eldest son. The family's brutal enforcer. | Leads the **blockade** and the **army**. Killed in the battle and dragged to Hell. |
| **Selene** | Daughter. The perfect host, and completely unhinged. | Hosts the **dinner**. Returns in the **battle**. **Escapes.** |
| **Andras** | Uncle. A respectable merchant on the surface, and the family's knife in the dark. | Strikes in the **tavern**. Haunts the **battle**. **Escapes.** |
| **Malphas** | Cousin. A hunter and a brute. | Fights in the **battle**. Leads the last attack on the **tower**. Killed by **Vartius**. |
| **Vashkar** | The butler. Secretly a rakshasa. | Spots the linked character. Saves Selene **twice**. Trapped in **Mirella's prism** and sent back to Hell. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0012"></div>


### Who Runs the Conspiracy

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0013"></div>


**Selene and Vashkar organise it together.**

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0014"></div>


- **Selene** is in charge on the ground, on Belial's orders: *secure Silverwood.* She is the public face: generous, gracious and always first to help.
- **Vashkar** is the planner at her side. He decides which problems to create, which to make worse and which to simply take advantage of, and he keeps the whole thing from falling apart. As Hell's handler, he is also steering it towards Lord Xanthos.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 34}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Blackwood family — GM dossier

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-BLACKWOOD-DOSSIER"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0015"></div>


The rest of the family carry out their parts:
- **Andras:** the money and the contracts.
- **Azazel and Malphas:** the muscle: the attacks, the bait and the threats.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0016"></div>


### Belial

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0017"></div>


The head of the family. Clever, patient and proud. He wants the family's wayward child **back in the business**. He doesn't want them dead, which is more than can be said for the rest of the family.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0018"></div>


### Naamah

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0019"></div>


Cold and unforgiving. To her, leaving the family is the worst betrayal there is. Selene takes after her.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0020"></div>


### Azazel

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0021"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-024-azazel-blackwood.webp" alt="Azazel Blackwood"/><div>Big, armoured and merciless, on a horse as vicious as he is. He made a bargain for his <b>infernal armour</b>, which drains life from anyone foolish enough to crowd him. When he dies, the armour <b>drags him down to Hell</b>.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0022"></div>


**Says:** *"Kneel."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0023"></div>


### Selene

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0024"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-022-selene-blackwood.webp" alt="Selene Blackwood"/><div>Elegant, gracious and impeccably mannered. You&#x27;d trust her to host a princess. She is also the most dangerous person in the family, because she enjoys it.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0025"></div>


**Says:** *"Oh, darling. Don't look so worried. It's only dinner."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0026"></div>


**Her orders, as she sees them.** Belial told her to secure Silverwood. When she spotted the linked character, he told her to deliver a message: *come home, the family has work for you.* She delivered it. But he never actually said **not to kill them**, so she decided she could.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0027"></div>


**In a fight,** she lashes out with a **whip of crackling magic**: part warlock, part street fighter.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0028"></div>


*For the guests' arrival at Selene's dinner, before her speech:*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 35}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Blackwood family — GM dossier

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-BLACKWOOD-DOSSIER"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0029"></div>


{{descriptive
Candlelight runs gold along polished timber. The table is laid for you: roast meats, fresh bread and silver cutlery that gleams. A servant stands behind every chair, eyes lowered, and two more wait by the doors. Selene Blackwood rises to greet you, every inch the perfect host. "Our heroes," she says warmly, and takes your hands one by one. In the corner, an elderly butler inclines his head, and doesn't blink.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0030"></div>


{{note
##### Her stat block
Selene's stat block is in the Bestiary: part warlock, part street fighter. Every Blackwood is there: **Azazel**, **Selene**, **Andras**, **Malphas** and **Vashkar**.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0031"></div>


### Andras

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0032"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-025-andras-blackwood.webp" alt="Andras Blackwood"/><div>On paper, Andras is the family&#x27;s legitimate face: a respectable merchant who keeps the books clean. In practice, he is <b>the knife in the night</b>.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0033"></div>


- He **teleports** in and out of fights.
- He wraps himself in **magical darkness** and sees perfectly through it.
- He strikes, and he's gone before anyone can react.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0034"></div>


He gets away at the end of the battle with Selene. He's the family's biggest loose end, and a good villain for a sequel.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0035"></div>


**Says:** *"Business is business. Nothing personal."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0036"></div>


### Malphas

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0037"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-026-malphas-blackwood.webp" alt="Malphas Blackwood"/><div>The family&#x27;s hunter. Sent with Azazel to bring the linked character home, one way or another. Where Andras is subtle, Malphas is a <b>brute</b>: armoured, strong and relentless.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0038"></div>


After the battle, he gathers what's left of the army and attacks Lord Xanthos's tower through the **portal room**. It's the last mistake he makes.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0039"></div>


**Says:** *"Found you."*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 36}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Blackwood family — GM dossier

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-BLACKWOOD-DOSSIER"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0040"></div>


### Vashkar

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0041"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-023-vashkar.webp" alt="Vashkar"/><div>An elderly, impeccably polite human butler, who has served the Blackwoods for longer than anyone can remember. He is really a <b>rakshasa</b>, Hell&#x27;s handler inside the family.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0042"></div>


- He watches Selene's madness calmly, because he's seen it all before.
- He only steps in to **protect an asset**: Selene at the dinner, and Selene again in the battle.
- Ordinary magic barely touches him. It takes **Mirella's prism** to bring him down.
- Killed outside the Hells, he simply **returns there** in time.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0043"></div>


**Says:** *"Will that be all, my lady?"*

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0044"></div>


### The Linked Character

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0045"></div>


One player character is tied to the Blackwoods, either raised in the household or caught up in its criminal business before running away (see *Session Zero*).

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0046"></div>


- **They don't know** the family is in Silverwood.
- **The butler spots them**, and the family's attention turns to them.
- **The party assumes the family just wants them home.** Right up until Selene's knife.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0047"></div>


If no player takes the link, use **Mordai**, a Blackwood defector the party befriends, as a last resort.

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0048"></div>


### How It Ends

<div class="swanchor" id="c13b0049"></div>


| Name | Fate |
|:--|:--|
| Belial and Naamah | Never seen. Still out there. |
| Azazel | Dragged to Hell in the battle. |
| Vashkar | Trapped in Mirella's prism and killed. Returns to Hell. |
| Malphas | Killed by Vartius at the tower. |
| Selene and Andras | **Escape.** |

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 37}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# Session Zero

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-SESSION-ZERO"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0001"></div>


## Before the caravan sets off

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0002"></div>


This campaign needs three things settled before play:

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0003"></div>


1. **A Blackwood link:** one character tied to the Blackwood family.
2. **Backstories:** permission to use each character's past in the story.
3. **Boundaries:** what the table is and isn't comfortable with.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0004"></div>


## 1. The Blackwood Link

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0005"></div>


**This campaign needs one character with a link to the Blackwood family.** The Silver Chapter depends on it.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0006"></div>


Offer one of the two backstory cards below to a player. Many players are happy to be handed a backstory, and some prefer it. If you're running pre-made characters, **one of them must have a Blackwood card.**

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0007"></div>


The cards contain **no spoilers**. They don't mention Silverwood, the mines or what the family is doing there.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0008"></div>


### Card A: The Runner

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0009"></div>


> *You grew up on the streets of a distant city, running messages, keeping watch and carrying packages you were told never to open, all for the Blackwoods. They are a wealthy, respected merchant family, and the most feared criminal syndicate in the city. They fed you, trained you and made you useful.*
>
> *Then one night you saw what was in one of those packages, and you ran. You've kept running ever since. You still remember the faces: Belial, the head of the family. His daughter Selene, who smiled while she did terrible things. And the old butler, Vashkar, who never seemed to miss anything.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0010"></div>


### Card B: The Adopted Child

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0011"></div>


> *You were taken in as a child by the Blackwoods, a wealthy tiefling family of merchants, and raised as one of their own. You grew up alongside their children: Azazel, the eldest, who was always cruel; Selene, who was charming and frightening in equal measure; and their cousin Malphas.*
>
> *As you grew older, you learned what the family really does. When they tried to bring you into it, you refused, and you left. Your mother, Naamah, said she would rather see you dead than disloyal. You believe she meant it.*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 38}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Session Zero

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-SESSION-ZERO"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0012"></div>


### How the Link Plays Out

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0013"></div>


1. **The name check.** The first time the Blackwoods are mentioned in Silverwood, the linked player may recognise the name. If they miss it, that's fine.
2. **The reveal.** In town, a well-dressed old man gives the linked character a strange glance and walks away. If the player asks *"Do I know him?"*, the answer is **yes: he's your family's butler, Vashkar.** This always happens, however oblivious the player is.
3. **The false comfort.** The party will likely assume the family just wants their relative home. Let them. When Selene's dinner invitation arrives, it looks like a family reunion.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0015"></div>


**Last resort: Mordai.** If nobody takes a card, use **Mordai Blackwood**, a defector the party befriends early in the Copper Chapter. Selene's speech and the attacks are aimed at him instead. It works, but it's weaker than when it's personal to a player.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0016"></div>


## 2. Backstories

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0017"></div>


Late in the campaign, the villain reveals that he **saved one of the characters, or their family, years ago**. It makes the final fight personal, and hard.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0018"></div>


**Don't give the twist away.** At session zero, say something like:

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0019"></div>


> *"Your backstory may come up and matter in this story. I'm planning to use it, unless you have any objections."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0020"></div>


If a player objects, leave their backstory alone.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0021"></div>


**If a player has a backstory,** look for a moment where a powerful wizard could have stepped in to save them or their family. Note it quietly.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0022"></div>


**If a player doesn't have one,** offer a hook from this table. Present it as part of their past, with no names.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 39}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Session Zero

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-SESSION-ZERO"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0023"></div>


| d6 | Hook |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | **The cult.** As a baby, you were nearly sacrificed by a cult. Priests of many faiths, and a wizard nobody could name, broke in and saved you. |
| 2 | **The slave ships.** You were taken by slavers. A wizard flew over the ships, broke your chains and sank them beneath the waves. |
| 3 | **The dragon.** Your parent died fighting a dragon in the mountains, alongside a wizard who survived. You were the only one of your people left. |
| 4 | **The plague.** Your village was dying of plague, until a wizard arrived with a cure and left without giving his name. |
| 5 | **The dead.** Undead overran your home. A wizard destroyed them and the thing that raised them. |
| 6 | **The portal.** Something came through a tear in the sky above your town. A wizard closed it, and nobody ever saw him again. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0024"></div>


**The payoff.** In the Mithril Chapter, Vartius tells each tied character the story, in detail, and reveals that the nameless wizard was **him**.

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0025"></div>


## 3. Boundaries

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0026"></div>


*Shadow over Silverwood* is a dark campaign in places. Before play, talk through these themes with the table:

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 40}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Session Zero

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="GM-SESSION-ZERO"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0027"></div>


| Theme | Where it appears | Softer option |
|:--|:--|:--|
| **Suicide** | The banshee's story (Silver) | Keep it in the past and off-screen. The lovers simply died of grief. |
| **Mutilated servants** | Selene's servants (Silver) | The servants are simply silent and terrified. |
| **Torture** | The Tormented Mages (Gold) | The mages are shackled captives forced to fight. |
| **Body theft** | Vartius's soul swaps (Platinum, Mithril) | Keep as is. It's the heart of the story. |
| **Imprisonment** | The tower captives (Mithril) | Keep descriptions brief. |
| **Forced breeding** | Vartius's bloodline programme (Mithril) | Refer to it only as "his bloodline experiments". |
| **Harm to children** | The children raised as future bodies (Mithril) | Keep them off-screen. The players only learn they exist. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c14b0028"></div>


**The story stays as written.** These options let a GM soften the details for their table without losing what makes the story hit. Agree what your group is comfortable with, and check in if things get heavy.

{{swpilot-note
##### SPOILER-CONSCIOUS PREPARATION
Keep player backstory cards free of investigation answers. Establish boundaries without disclosing twists. Bed-warmer scenes fade to black; immunity/resistance and exhaustion remain unchanged and are not a sales menu. Sensitive-scene references: [Shaft and Pickaxe · p. 59](#p59), Silver banshee/crypt, Gold poisoning and Platinum/Mithril tower.
}}

If a player overlooks a name already in their backstory, use an appropriate reminder; do not manufacture a new revelation or replace the existing fallback.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 41}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# The last caravan camp

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="PRO-CARAVAN"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0001"></div>


## Level 2 · One session

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0002"></div>


The adventurers have been hired to guard a **Farman food caravan** on the long road to Silverwood: carts of vegetables, salted meat and grain for a town that can't feed itself. At the last camp before Silverwood, goblins attack.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0003"></div>


The Prologue teaches combat, introduces the region and brings the caravan to Silverwood’s town threshold. Guild registration, payment, level 3 and Copper Passes open the Copper Chapter.

<figure class="swcrop-left"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/art-f58a502c35-sunset-caravan-through-misty-mountains.webp" alt="Sunset Caravan Through Misty Mountains"/></figure>

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0004"></div>


{{note
##### Before you start
Run **session zero** first. This campaign needs **one character with a link to the Blackwood family** (see *Session Zero*). Make sure that link is in place before the caravan sets off.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0005"></div>


### The Caravan

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0006"></div>


Carts like these reach Silverwood **once a week**. Once unloaded, the same carts carry mythril ore back out under guard.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0007"></div>


**Bram Tallow** *(human, Farman caravan master).* Weathered, practical and tired of goblins. He hired the party because his regular guards keep quitting. He knows the road, the town and the price of everything.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0008"></div>


**Says:** *"Goblins. Course it's goblins. It's always goblins."*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 42}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The last caravan camp

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="PRO-CARAVAN"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0009"></div>


### Encounter: Arrows in the Dark

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0010"></div>


**The camp.** The caravan has stopped where the road splits, a junction shaped like a capital **F**:
- The **top road** climbs north to Silverwood.
- The **middle road** runs east towards the goblin lands.
- The **bottom road** leads south, back the way they came.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0011"></div>


The camp sits in the top-left corner of the junction, wagons circled around a dying fire.

{{swpilot-note
##### SET THE SCENE
Bram has travelled with the characters for about two weeks. This is the last camp, less than one day’s onward travel from Silverwood. Establish who is on watch, who is asleep and where each character is before the arrows. Other guards have their own responsibilities.
}}

The hungry goblins reach the Eastern Path from the Goblin Hills by a small route through the mountains that only they can naturally negotiate. This explains the attack; it is not a new party shortcut. Use [the regional bearings · p. 25](#p25) and map M-22 for the campsite.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 43}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# Arrows in the Dark

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="PRO-ATTACK"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0012"></div>


{{descriptive
A wet thud, then another: arrows hitting flesh and leather. Someone outside the tents screams. High, cackling voices rise from the darkness, and the camp erupts.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0013"></div>


**The goblins.** Six **goblins**, plus one more for each character beyond four. That's a **hard** fight for level 2 on paper, but goblins that run off with food aren't fighting. Caravan guards fight their own battles at the edges of the camp, so the party only deals with these.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0014"></div>


**What the goblins want: the food.** They are hungry, not suicidal. Each round, any goblin next to a cart can spend its turn grabbing a sack and running east down the middle road.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0015"></div>


- **Goblins that escape with food** reduce the party's pay (see *Payment*).
- Once half the goblins are down, the rest grab what they can and flee.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0016"></div>


{{note
##### What this fight teaches
- **It's dark.** Goblins see in the dark and many characters don't. Light matters.
- **Objectives matter.** The goblins aren't trying to win. They're trying to steal. Stopping them is as important as killing them.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="SRD51-GOBLIN"></div>


{{monster,frame
## Goblin
*Small humanoid (goblinoid), neutral evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 15 (leather armor, shield)
**Hit Points** :: 7 (2d6)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
| STR | DEX | CON | INT | WIS | CHA |
|:--:|:--:|:--:|:--:|:--:|:--:|
| 8 (-1) | 14 (+2) | 10 (+0) | 10 (+0) | 8 (-1) | 8 (-1) |
___
**Skills** :: Stealth +6
**Senses** :: darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 9
**Languages** :: Common, Goblin
**Challenge** :: 1/4 (50 XP)
___
***Nimble Escape.*** The goblin can take the Disengage or Hide action as a bonus action on each of its turns.
### Actions
***Scimitar.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 5 (1d6 + 2) slashing damage.
***Shortbow.*** *Ranged Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, range 80/320 ft., one target. *Hit:* 5 (1d6 + 2) piercing damage.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0017"></div>


**After the fight.** One of the caravan guards wanders over, wiping his blade.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0018"></div>


> *"Take their ears. The Guild in Silverwood pays a bounty for goblins."*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 44}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Arrows in the Dark

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="PRO-ATTACK"></div>



{{swpilot-note
##### ENCOUNTER DESK
Four characters: six goblins. Six characters: eight goblins. Keep a running count of sacks carried off, goblins down and goblins retreating. The other guards are occupied elsewhere in the camp; no new reinforcement round or automatic surprise is added. Use the party’s actual watches and light.
}}

| Record | Count / notes |
|:--|:--|
| Food sacks stolen | __________ |
| Goblins down / escaped | __________ |
| Ears retained for Guild payment | __________ |
| Guards / cargo / character consequences | __________ |

}}

# Silverwood at the threshold

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="PRO-APPROACH"></div>



<figure class="swpilot-scene"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/silverwood-l1.webp" alt="Silverwood — accepted ground level"/></figure>

Full town plans: [L0 · p. 47](#silverwood-atlas-l0) · [L1 · p. 48](#silverwood-atlas-l1) · [L2 · p. 49](#silverwood-atlas-l2) · [L3 · p. 50](#silverwood-atlas-l3) · [L4 · p. 51](#silverwood-atlas-l4).


<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0019"></div>


### Arrival

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0020"></div>


The following day, the caravan completes the remaining journey and reaches Silverwood’s **north gate**.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0021"></div>


{{descriptive
Silverwood clings to the side of the mountain like moss on a stone: walls, towers and houses stacked on houses, stairways and bridges threaded between them. Even at this hour the streets are busy. Miners heading out, carts coming in, voices in a dozen accents. Above it all, on the mountain, a lone tower catches the first light.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0022"></div>


**The gate.** Two weathered statues flank the north gate, carved so well into the wall that most people never notice them. A character who rolls a **natural 20** on a Perception check, or who specifically studies them, realises they aren't decoration. They're **stone golems**, dormant.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0036"></div>


The Prologue ends at the town threshold. Continue with [the party’s first Guild registration · p. 56](#p56) in Copper.

}}

{{sweyebrow SILVERWOOD / GM ATLAS}}

{{swbody

# Reading Silverwood’s levels

## One town, five floors

North is up on all five plans. Buildings, gates, canals, the Bazaar shaft and the Guild’s southwest tower keep the same positions from floor to floor. Reveal only the spaces the party has discovered.

| Level | Main use |
|:--|:--|
| [L0 · p. 47](#silverwood-atlas-l0) | Sewers and the Bazaar pit/holding complex |
| [L1 · p. 48](#silverwood-atlas-l1) | Gaming, kitchens, private booths, shops, forge and barracks |
| [L2 · p. 49](#silverwood-atlas-l2) | Main tavern/stage, Guild administration, Pantheon and upper rooms |
| [L3 · p. 50](#silverwood-atlas-l3) | Guild meeting/lodging, Halls gallery and homes |
| [L4 · p. 51](#silverwood-atlas-l4) | Highest occupied rooms and lower roofs |

## Landmarks and approaches

The northwest Bazaar’s round crane tower, rectangular hall and outer wall form one structure. The crane lifts creatures from the northern plateau to the handling shaft and pits. It does not collect them from the western cliff.

The Adventurers’ Guild occupies the southwest block; its southwest corner tower continues through the occupied floors. The forge meets the eastern mountain. The smaller west gate reaches a tiny stone outcrop and a dilapidated broken bridge stub.

The town is about seven centuries old. Joined houses, altered footprints, repaired walls and irregular paths are part of its history.

## Cross-floor use

Use the repeated stair positions and short upper walkways to follow movement. Muted lower buildings and terrain provide context beneath the active floor. These illustrated plans are a guide to the town, not a measured tactical grid.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM ATLAS}}

{{pageNumber 52}}
\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# The Copper Chapter

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-COVER"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0001"></div>


## Levels 3–4

Find your feet in Silverwood: meet its people, choose work and discover the pattern behind the mine troubles. [Open the facing-page GM desk · p. 54](#p54).

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

# Copper at a Glance

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-GLANCE"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0002"></div>


The party has arrived, registered and been let loose on Silverwood. This chapter is about **finding their feet**: exploring the town, meeting its people, taking their first jobs, and noticing that something is wrong in the mines.

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0004"></div>


**GM only.** The Blackwoods are quietly undermining every other house: causing some accidents, making real ones worse and letting the blame fall elsewhere. Each time, they step in to help, and each "rescue" becomes a contract, a debt or a loyal worker. They don't need to own the mines. They need the mines to be **unable to run without them**.

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0005"></div>


At this stage, **nobody suspects them**. The evidence points at other houses. The players should end this chapter with a growing feeling that the Blackwoods are *always there*, without being able to prove anything yet.

{{swpilot-note
##### RANK / OBJECTIVE / ENDING
Arrive at level 2; registration grants level 3 and Copper Passes. Copper normally covers levels 3–4. The council vote starts Silver’s story whichever way it goes; Silver Passes require level 5 and a genuine mining lead.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0007"></div>


There is no set order. The party can:
- Explore the town and meet its people (see the town entries)
- Take on **Operation Scratch and Sniff**, Faldor's secret investigation (given on their first day)
- Visit **Mirella**, as Faldor told them to, and hear her prophecy
- Take **jobs from the board**

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0008"></div>


The **trouble in the mines carries on** whether the party investigates or not (see *The Village Diary*).

{{swpilot-note
##### ACTIVE WORLD CLOCK
Day 1 is 20 Verdantis 1494. The Village Diary keeps moving. Choose the written third-fall branch from actual actions; record the vote, evidence, level and credential separately. Faldor, Sariel, the Mining Council and the apparently helpful Blackwoods are pivotal.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 54}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

| Rumours, games and jobs | [Social play · p. 90](#p90) / [Job desk · p. 99](#p99) |

##### ACCESS ≠ CHALLENGE
Town and ordinary local work are accessible. A goblin camp alarm can be deadly; the swamps, night Obelisk and deep Feywoods can exceed Copper’s capabilities. The party can go there: use warnings, early-visit material and the relevant later chapter, not an invisible rank barrier.
}}

{{swpilot-note
##### AT EVERY VENUE
**First arrival once** · **repeat visit freeform** · **event dialogue by prerequisite**. For a first-ever visit at a later rank, use the Copper introduction, then the current chapter’s changed state.
}}

# First Guild registration

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-REGISTRATION"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0023"></div>


Bram points the party up through the town to the **Shaft and Pickaxe**: *"Guild's on the second floor. Tell Sariel I sent you."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0024"></div>


### At the Guild

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0025"></div>


**Sariel Windrider** registers the party. Read or paraphrase:

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0026"></div>


>*"Welcome to the Silverwood Adventurers' Guild! I am the Guild administrator, Sariel. As Bram mentioned, I will be registering you with the town and issuing you Guild Passes for the area. These will let you take on jobs from the board and adventure in and around Silverwood.*
>
> *I also want to make you aware that your passes are enchanted with a detect life spell and a locate object spell. This is for your safety, in case of any unfortunate accidents. If your life force is extinguished while on a job, the pass will notify the Guild so we can recover your remains. It's a precaution, but it's important for us to have it in place.*
>
> *I also wanted to thank you for your help in getting the food to Silverwood. Here is your payment. Well done!"*

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0027"></div>


She then pays them for the caravan job and the goblin ears, and gives them their **Copper Passes**.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0028"></div>


**Payment**
- **Caravan escort:** 25 gp each, minus 2 gp each for every sack of food the goblins stole.
- **Goblin bounty:** 1 gp per pair of ears.

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0029"></div>


**Level up.** The characters reach **level 3**.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 56}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# First Guild registration

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-REGISTRATION"></div>



{{swpilot-note
##### EXPLAIN THE PASS BEFORE WARNINGS
Pass order: Copper, Silver, Gold, Platinum, Mithril. Copper covers levels 3–4, Silver 5–6, Gold 7–8, Platinum 9–10 and Mithril 11+. These are challenge and credential guides, not physical travel gates. The pass can be located and reports its owner’s death. Full terms and costs: [Guild reference · p. 26](#p26).
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0030"></div>


### Orientation

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0031"></div>


Sariel gives the party a quick tour of what Silverwood offers. Read or paraphrase:

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0032"></div>


> *"The Shaft and Pickaxe downstairs will feed you and keep you in drink. The Hallowed Halls offer healing and blessings, whoever your god. The Miner's Mart sells everything practical. Ironclad's for weapons and armour. Tormund's Bizarre Bazaar buys and sells the stranger things. Mirella's Mystic Emporium is just across the bridge for anything magical. And the Apothecary sells potions, but the owner can be a bit grumpy at times.*
>
> *However, please be careful when venturing out beyond the walls. The Xanthos Crypt to the north, the goblin village to the east and the enchanted forest to the west and southwest are dangerous areas. They are better suited to Silver Pass adventurers.*
>
> *Do you have any questions or concerns before you begin your adventures?"*

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0033"></div>


Before they can leave, Sariel adds: *"Oh, and the Guildmaster wants a word. Upstairs. Don't keep him waiting."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c15b0037"></div>


{{note
##### What's happened behind the scenes
**Mirella** watched the party come up the road and sent word to Faldor that they're not part of Silverwood's troubles. That's why a grumpy Guildmaster trusts a brand-new party on their first day.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 57}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# Faldor, then Sariel

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-COMMISSION"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0110"></div>


## Operation Scratch and Sniff

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0111"></div>


### Day One: Faldor

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0112"></div>


This happens **on the party's first day**, straight after they register (see Guild Registration). Mirella watched them come up the road and sent word to Faldor before they even reached the gate. He calls them up to his office.

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0113"></div>


**Faldor is grumpy, blunt and short.** This is the longest conversation the party will ever have with him. Read or paraphrase:

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0114"></div>


> *"Sit. Don't touch anything. Mirella vouches for you lot. That tricksy witch is hardly ever wrong, more's the pity.*
>
> *Something's wrong in that mining village. Accidents, lost wagons, houses at each other's throats, and every week someone new offering to help, while it all gets worse. I don't know who's behind it. Maybe nobody. I want people with no house to answer to keeping their eyes open. Quietly. No accusations. Just bring me what you see.*
>
> *I'm calling it Operation Scratch and Sniff."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0115"></div>


He looks very pleased with the name. When the players inevitably ask *"Mirella who?"*:

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0116"></div>


> *"Hmph. That witch is definitely up to something, and now you're in it. When you're done here, go and see her. And go and see Sariel. Tell her it's Scratch and Sniff."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0117"></div>


{{note
##### Faldor and Mirella
Faldor finds Mirella's powers deeply unsettling. He still doesn't trust her completely, but he has learned the hard way that she's usually right.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0118"></div>


### Day One: Sariel

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0119"></div>


**Sariel** sighs at the name. *"I should never have let him name it. Don't ever say that out loud. Anywhere."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0120"></div>


She has **no jobs for them today**:

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0121"></div>


> *"I'll have work lined up for you tomorrow. Explore the town, get settled, and see me in the morning."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c16b0122"></div>


{{note
##### Faldor is the face, Sariel is the brains
Faldor is the face of the Guild, and players will rarely get more than a sentence out of him. **Sariel runs it**, and she knows far more than he does. Players who befriend her get better jobs, better rumours and better answers.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 58}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# The Shaft and Pickaxe

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-SHAFT"></div>



{{swpilot-note
##### FIRST CONTACT / RETURN HUB
Use this entry on the first actual visit. Later visits use the people and portrayal notes. Guild registration and the first-day commission are once-only scenes. The Greenhart menu is safe before the blockade; the Gold food clock has not begun.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0001"></div>


## Tavern and Adventurers' Guild

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0002"></div>


*Rising through three of Silverwood's tiers, the Shaft and Pickaxe is two things at once: the loudest tavern in town and the home of the Adventurers' Guild. Miners drink downstairs, adventurers argue over the job board one floor up, and somewhere above it all a gruff dwarf decides who is ready for what.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0003"></div>


### Layout

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0004"></div>


| Floor | What's here |
|:--|:--|
| **Lower tier** | The **gambling and games** level, the **kitchens** and the stores. Entrance from the lower street. |
| **Middle tier** | The **Shaft and Pickaxe** itself: the bar, long tables, a **stage** where Omega plays, the fireplace, a **private reserved area**, the registration desk and the **job board**, with bay windows over the town. A round **tower** rises from one corner. A **bridge** leads across to Mirella's Mystic Emporium. |
| **Upper tier** | The **Guild offices** and storage, a meeting room, and the members' lodgings, sorted by pass: **Copper rooms**, **Silver rooms**, **Gold rooms** and a **VIP suite**. The tower climbs on to a third floor. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0005"></div>


Internal staircases join all three floors. Outside, the building is reached by the crooked lanes, stairs and bridges that run through the whole town.

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0006"></div>


{{note
##### Running the Shaft and Pickaxe
This is where most sessions start and end. Players return here to hand in jobs, collect pay, hear rumours and rank up. Let it feel like home: the same faces, the same jokes, the same table in the corner.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 59}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Shaft and Pickaxe

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-SHAFT"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0007"></div>


### The People

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0008"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/npc-001-faldor-ironhewer-canon.webp" alt="Faldor Ironhewer"/><div><b>Faldor Ironhewer</b> <i>(dwarf, Guildmaster).</i> A grizzled, bearded dwarf with a stern expression, poring over contracts at a desk too big for his office. Grumpy, blunt and short with everyone. He is the <b>face</b> of the Guild, and players will rarely get more than a sentence out of him. He doesn&#x27;t hand out a better pass until he&#x27;s sure you won&#x27;t die with it. He suspects something is wrong in the mines. He finds Mirella&#x27;s powers unsettling, but he&#x27;s learned she&#x27;s usually right.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0009"></div>


**Says:** *"Hmph. You're not dead. Good. Don't make a habit of it."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0010"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/npc-002-sariel-windrider-canon.webp" alt="Sariel Windrider"/><div><b>Sariel Windrider</b> <i>(half-elf, Guild administrator).</i> Short dark hair, bright green eyes and a warm smile. She greets visitors at the front desk, answers questions and points them to the right people. <b>She actually runs the Guild</b>, and she knows far more than Faldor does. Everyone knows the Guild is really run by Sariel. Nobody dares say it, with Faldor&#x27;s temper. Players who befriend her get better jobs, better rumours and better answers.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0011"></div>


**Says:** *"Sign here, here and here. No, not there. Here."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0012"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-003-kethra.webp" alt="Kethra"/><div><b>Kethra</b> <i>(tiefling, librarian).</i> A quiet tiefling with long black hair and sharp features. She keeps the guild library: maps, historical tomes, bestiaries and anything else useful to an adventurer.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0013"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-004-grimgar.webp" alt="Grimgar"/><div><b>Grimgar</b> <i>(half-orc, tavern owner).</i> Burly and gruff, with a shaved head and a thick beard. He stands behind the bar polishing mugs and grunts a greeting at customers. He loves Pippin, and doesn&#x27;t even realise it. He hears everything said across his bar.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0014"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-005-pippin.webp" alt="Pippin"/><div><b>Pippin</b> <i>(halfling, barmaid).</i> Petite, with curly brown hair and mischievous green eyes. She flits between tables with surprising speed, taking orders and making small talk. If something happened in Silverwood last night, Pippin knows by breakfast. The one thing she hasn&#x27;t noticed is how Grimgar feels about her.</div></div>

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 60}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Shaft and Pickaxe

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-SHAFT"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0015"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/npc-006-lily-canon.webp" alt="Lily"/><div><b>Lily</b> <i>(human, barmaid).</i> Long blonde hair, bright blue eyes, and a friendly smile as she takes orders and delivers drinks.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0016"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/npc-007-thalion-canon.webp" alt="Thalion"/><div><b>Thalion</b> <i>(drow, cook).</i> Tall and lean, with long silver hair pulled back in a ponytail. He comes out of the kitchen with trays of hot food, gives the room a nod, and disappears again. His family may have roots in the old Feywoods, and his cooking shows it.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0017"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/npc-008-omega-oliver-canon.webp" alt="Omega Oliver"/><div><b>Omega Oliver</b> <i>(half-elf, bard).</i> Plays most nights in the Shaft and Pickaxe. His songs, riddles and old tales hide real clues about the region&#x27;s history. Players who pay for a story often get more than they bargained for. He loves a puzzle, and he&#x27;s the best person in town to help <b>decode</b> anything written in riddles.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0018"></div>


**Flubs** is never far from him: a cute **rainbow acid slime** that wobbles, bounces and bubbles. Flubs can't hurt anyone, but it makes little **illusions** (colours, faces, tiny fireworks) to go with Omega's songs, and it loves to hide in people's bags.

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0043"></div>


### The Two Menus

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0044"></div>


Silverwood can't grow enough food, and supplies arrive by caravan only once a week. So from the party's first day, the tavern offers **two menus**:

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0045"></div>


| The Regular Menu | Price |
|:--|:--|
| **Hunter's Stew:** venison or other game, potatoes and root vegetables | 10 gp |
| **Trout and Wild Greens:** freshly caught trout with wild greens and herbs | 8 gp |
| **Mushroom and Barley Soup:** wild mushrooms, barley and root vegetables, with bread | 6 gp |
| **Roasted Wild Boar:** with root vegetables and a savoury sauce | 20 gp |
| **Foraged Fruit Tart:** foraged berries and fruit, with whipped cream | 7 gp |
| **Grilled Acorn Squash:** filled with wild rice, mushrooms and herbs | 12 gp |

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 61}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Shaft and Pickaxe

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-SHAFT"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0046"></div>


| The Greenhart Menu | Price |
|:--|:--|
| **Spiced Kiwi Salad:** sliced spiky kiwi, mixed greens and a spicy dressing | 5 sp |
| **Blossoming Squash Soup:** creamy, with root vegetables and herbs, and bread | 4 sp |
| **Cactus Fruit and Succulent Berry Smoothie:** sweet and tangy, with ice and honey | 3 sp |
| **Carob Pod and Sunflower Seed Bread:** served warm with butter | 2 sp |
| **Gourd and Root Vegetable Stew:** in a savoury broth, with bread | 5 sp |
| **Mossy Apple and Bamboo Rice Pudding:** topped with whipped cream | 4 sp |

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0047"></div>


The regular menu is expensive because everything on it comes up the road by caravan. That's deliberate: it makes the cheap menu tempting.

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0048"></div>


The Greenhart food comes from **Lord Xanthos's magical greenhouse**, a gift to the town. It's cheap, plentiful and delicious, and most miners eat nothing else.

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0049"></div>


*Handout: the two menus (see the Handout Pack).*

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0050"></div>


{{note
##### For now, it's safe
Greenhart food has **no effects at all** until the **Gold Chapter**, when the blockade forces the greenhouse to grow faster and the food turns. The two menus are simply a choice of price, for now.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0051"></div>


### Bed Warmers

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0052"></div>


For **5 gp a night**, a guest at the Shaft and Pickaxe needn't sleep alone. The tavern's bed warmers are **Valeria** *(human)*, **Rowan** *(half-elf)*, **Darian** *(tiefling)*, **Elena** *(half-elf)* and **Lucas** *(human)*. Keep it behind a closed door: what happens is up to the table, and fading to black is always fine.

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0053"></div>


A good night leaves a character with a spring in their step. Roll a d10 the next morning. Every boon lasts **12 hours** unless stated, and when it ends, the character gains **one level of exhaustion**.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 62}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# The Shaft and Pickaxe

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-SHAFT"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0054"></div>


| d10 | Boon |
|:--:|:--|
| 1 | Immunity to one damage type of the player's choice |
| 2 | +10 feet of speed |
| 3 | Resistance to one damage type of the player's choice |
| 4 | Advantage on checks with one skill of the player's choice |
| 5 | Temporary hit points equal to your level × 2, until your next long rest |
| 6 | Advantage on saving throws of one ability of the player's choice |
| 7 | +2 damage on weapon and spell attacks |
| 8 | Your hit point maximum rises by your level until your next long rest |
| 9 | Advantage on initiative rolls |
| 10 | +1 to your spell save DC |

<div class="swanchor" id="c04b0055"></div>


{{note
##### Darian
Darian is **Darian Gray**, a Blackwood man. He listens to pillow talk and reports it back. When the Blackwoods leave Silverwood, **Darian disappears** too.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 63}}

# Mirella’s Mystic Emporium

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-MIRELLA"></div>



{{swpilot-note
##### FIRST CONTACT / ONGOING PORTRAYAL
First arrival once, at any rank. Repeat visits use freeform portrayal and the current chapter’s actual changes; event speeches wait for their prerequisites. The prophecy is once only. Play Mirella slowly, graciously and reliably, with a faint knowing smirk; no innuendo or forced attraction. Her smoke familiars do not make her omniscient.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0001"></div>


## Magic, books and things best not asked about

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0002"></div>


*The first thing you notice is the smoke: thick, hazy and an iridescent purple that shimmers and pulses with a strange energy. It doesn't hide anything. You can see clearly around you, but you get the sense you're being watched from every angle.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0003"></div>


*Tall bookshelves line the walls, full of dusty tomes and grimoires bound in leather and embossed with gold. Tables and counters hold potions, wands, scrolls and arcane curiosities. In one corner, a young apprentice scribbles furiously in a journal. Soft music plays, and the air smells of sweet incense.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0004"></div>


### Layout

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0005"></div>


| Floor | What's here |
|:--|:--|
| **Ground** | A narrow stair up from the street. Easy to miss, which suits her regulars. |
| **Middle** | The shop: magic items, books, curiosities and Mirella's reading table. The **bridge** from the Guild hall arrives here. |
| **Upper** | Mirella's private rooms. Guests are invited, never assumed. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0006"></div>


### Mirella

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0007"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-010-mirella.webp" alt="Mirella"/><div><i>Tiefling, owner, diviner.</i> Jet-black hair, piercing green eyes and deep crimson skin. She carries an aura of power and confidence, and every movement is graceful and fluid. She takes slow, deliberate puffs on a long pipe and breathes the smoke out in a sultry cloud.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0008"></div>


Her magic comes off her as thick purple smoke, an extension of her and her senses. She often shapes it into cats and sends them out across town, which makes her a master of information, and the butt of many a pussy joke from those of a cruder nature. She's known throughout the land as a master of magic. Her prices are high, and her customers know they're paying for quality. She's a shrewd businesswoman and not to be trifled with: anyone who tries to cheat her is swiftly dealt with, with a flick of her wrist and a muttered incantation.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0009"></div>


**Says:** *"Sit, dear one. You're about to ask me something you already know the answer to."*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 64}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Mirella’s Mystic Emporium

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-MIRELLA"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0010"></div>


**How to play her.** Mirella is magnetic without trying to be. The charm is in how she moves, speaks and smokes, never in what she says. Play her slow and amused, a little too knowing, and always one step ahead of the conversation. Nearly every player will love her. One will distrust her, and that's fine too.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0011"></div>


**What she wants.** Silverwood safe, and to know everything happening in it. She is a sharp businesswoman with high prices, and she does not tolerate cheats.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0012"></div>


**What she is not.** She is not all-knowing, and she isn't anyone's exposition machine. She sees glimpses, not answers.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0013"></div>


### The Smoke Familiars

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0014"></div>


With a breath of purple smoke, Mirella shapes animals she can see and hear through: cats, crows and sometimes stranger things. They have fur, feathers and weight. They purr, caw and scratch.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0015"></div>


Her smoke cats wander the whole town, which makes her **the best-informed person in Silverwood** and a real problem for anyone with secrets. The Blackwoods know it, and later in the campaign they take steps against her.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0016"></div>


{{note
##### Her smoke in the story
Mirella's smoke turns up at key moments:
- **Copper:** she watched the party come up the road, and vouches for them to Faldor.
- **Gold:** a smoke crow finds the blockade on the Eastern Path.
- **Gold:** at the war council she blows the Eastern Path and the army across the table in miniature.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0017"></div>


### First Meeting: the Prophecy

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0018"></div>


The first time the party visits, the smoke wraps around them before she speaks. It seems to hold them protectively, as if they're being watched over and guided. Read or paraphrase:

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0019"></div>


> *"Welcome, travellers. I have been expecting you, for I have seen glimpses of your journey in my dreams.*
>
> *I offer no gifts, but I can provide guidance and aid where I can. Through the threads of fate, I have glimpsed the potential within you."*

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0020"></div>


With a wave of her hand, she conjures a shimmering image of Silverwood in the smoke, its forests teeming with life. Then a shadow creeps across it.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0021"></div>


> *"There is a shadow over Silverwood. The fate of this town hangs in the balance, and it is you who hold the power to shape its destiny. But remember, every choice you make has consequences, and the path you choose will not be without its trials.*
>
> *Will you accept my guidance, dear ones?"*

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 65}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Mirella’s Mystic Emporium

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-MIRELLA"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0022"></div>


Before the party even reached the gate, Mirella had already sent word to **Faldor** that these newcomers aren't part of Silverwood's troubles. That's why he trusts them with the quiet job in the mining village, and why he sends them to her.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0023"></div>


### What She Sells

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0024"></div>


| Stock | Notes |
|:--|:--|
| **Magic items** | Fine quality, high prices. |
| **Herb books** | Each covers a handful of plants, like her copy of the *Herbarum Pistrix* (see the herb pages in the *Handout Pack*). Owning the right book gives **advantage on checks to find and harvest those plants.** No reading or tracking needed: just own it. |
| **Blueprint books** | Old designs for weapons, armour and gear, beyond what Ironclad's has. Used with *Arcane Forge*'s crafting rules. |
| **Monster books** | Notes on local creatures: habits, weaknesses and warning signs. One describes a shadowy creature that drains strength, *"and when your strength is gone, so are you."* Players who read it will recognise the **mirror shades** much later. |
| **Readings** | A glimpse of what may come. Never a straight answer. |
| **Research** | For a fee, she looks into an object, a name or a rumour. It takes time. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0025"></div>


| Item | Price |
|:--|:--|
| Herb book (covers a handful of plants) | 25 gp |
| Monster book | 50 gp |
| Blueprint book | By the design: use *Arcane Forge*'s prices |
| A reading | 10 gp |
| Research | 25 gp per day |

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0026"></div>


**Her magic stock** grows as the party's Guild pass does:

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 66}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Mirella’s Mystic Emporium

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-MIRELLA"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0027"></div>


| Pass | Items |
|:--|:--|
| Copper | Potion of healing (50 gp), scroll of *magic missile* (50 gp), scroll of *sleep* (50 gp), bag of tricks (100 gp), rope of climbing (100 gp), eversmoking bottle (250 gp), sending stones (250 gp) |
| Silver and Gold | Bag of holding (500 gp), amulet of health (500 gp), necklace of fireballs (700 gp), cloak of protection (700 gp), ring of protection (1,000 gp), ring of jumping (1,000 gp) |
| Platinum and Mithril | Cloak of elvenkind (2,500 gp), gloves of missile snaring (2,500 gp), staff of the woodlands (4,000 gp), pearl of power (4,000 gp), staff of charming (6,000 gp), tome of understanding (6,000 gp) |

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0028"></div>


She also offers **enchantment services** for weapons and armour.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0029"></div>


*Mirella doesn't haggle. Anyone who tries to cheat her finds her prices go up.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0030"></div>


### Jobs and Hooks

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0031"></div>


- **Her Gathering job** on the Guild board wants the parts of a juvenile manticore (Copper Chapter). Her **herb books** help with Ampersand's Gathering job.
- **Unusual orders:** odd ingredients, a lost book, something fetched from a place nobody visits.
- **The bard's riddles** sometimes send players to her for help decoding them.
- **Mirella and Ampersand** are both beloved, but they do different jobs. Mirella knows things. Ampersand makes things.

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0032"></div>


### Rumours About Mirella (d4)

<div class="swanchor" id="c05b0033"></div>


| d4 | Rumour |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | "She knew my name before I said it." |
| 2 | "Her cats aren't real cats. Watch one walk through a wall sometime." |
| 3 | "She's older than she looks. Much older." |
| 4 | "Someone tried to cheat her once. Nobody's seen him since. Well, nobody's seen *all* of him." |

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE}}

# Ampersand’s Apothecary

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-AMPERSAND"></div>



{{swpilot-note
##### FIRST CONTACT / ONGOING PORTRAYAL
First arrival once, at any rank. Repeat visits use freeform portrayal and the current chapter’s actual changes; event speeches wait for their prerequisites. Zoltan becomes extremely welcoming when the party agrees to test potions whose effects he does not know; ordinary sales stay with Valor-33.
}}

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0000"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0001"></div>


## Alchemist and potions

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0002"></div>


*A small, dimly lit shop, shelves lined with jars and bottles full of strange substances. Behind the counter stands a tall clockwork construct in polished metal plates. Somewhere out the back, something bubbles, something glows, and something has just gone bang. Nobody in the shop reacts.*

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0003"></div>


### Zoltan "Ampersand" Ambersand

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0004"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/main/Silverwood/production-2026-10-25/npc-011-zoltan-ampersand-ambersand.webp" alt="Zoltan Ampersand Ambersand"/><div><i>Gnome, alchemist.</i> A small, wiry gnome with a wild shock of curly hair and bright, piercing eyes. He spends long hours in his cluttered laboratory, surrounded by bubbling flasks and strange, glowing powders.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0005"></div>


Zoltan is a little odd. He mutters to himself and scribbles incoherent notes in his journal. But he's a skilled alchemist with a huge knowledge of rare and exotic ingredients, and adventurers swear by his potions.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0006"></div>


**He is not a welcoming gnome.** He rarely deals with customers, and stays holed up in his lab. Prices are on a sign out front, and customers are expected to leave payment in a small box by the door. Lucky for everyone, his clockwork butler faces the public.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0007"></div>


Those who take the time to get to know him find a brilliant mind and a deep passion for his craft.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0008"></div>


**How he talks.** Zoltan talks about himself in the third person, and he never stops selling:

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0009"></div>


> *"Ah, adventurers, you have come to visit Ampersand, the alchemist! Ampersand has brewed a new concoction, yes yes, a potion of ghost poo's, that will take you on a journey like no other! No no, with just a sip, you will be able to leave your body and float above your excrement, no matter where it may be! And fear not, for Ampersand has made sure this potion is as safe as a baby bird in its nest. So come, come, and let Ampersand show you the magic of his creations!"*

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0010"></div>


**Why "Ampersand"?** Zoltan comes from the Ambersands, a wealthy family of apothecaries. His experiments got bigger, stranger and louder until one blew up the family laboratory, and they finally disowned him. On the road to Silverwood everyone misheard his name as "Ampersand", and the sign-painter made the same mistake. He kept it.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 68}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Ampersand’s Apothecary

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-AMPERSAND"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0011"></div>


### Valor-33

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0012"></div>


<div class="swbio"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Omegaoliverid/Public-images/67124c193e8b5fa3c01f0682bcd24c4230fa89a3/Silverwood/book-continuity-2026-10-29/npc-012-valor-33-canon.webp" alt="Valor-33"/><div><i>Clockwork construct, butler.</i> The stalwart servant of the Ambersand family, tall and built from sturdy metal plates, with generations of loyal service behind him. He came with Zoltan when the family threw him out, and nobody&#x27;s sure whether he was sent to look after him or chose to.</div></div>

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0013"></div>


Valor-33 runs the counter, takes the money and answers the questions. He's obedient, mostly. Every so often he answers back, and gently scolds the young master when his antics go too far.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0014"></div>


**Says:** *"The young master is busy. The young master is always busy. What do you need?"*

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0015"></div>


### The Perfectly Portable Pop-Up Brewery

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0016"></div>


Zoltan's pride and joy: a folding laboratory that packs into a satchel, with a gem set in an ampersand-shaped clasp. He takes it everywhere, which means he can brew almost anywhere, including places he really shouldn't.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0017"></div>


### What He Sells

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0018"></div>


| Item | Price |
|:--|:--|
| Potion of healing | 50 gp |
| Potion of greater healing (Silver Pass or above) | 150 gp |
| Common potions (whatever didn't explode this week) | 25–100 gp |
| Experimental potion (roll on the table below) | 10 gp |
| Ingredients | Bought and sold, especially anything rare or odd |
| Identifying a potion (Zoltan tastes it) | 5 gp |

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0019"></div>


### Potion Testing

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0020"></div>


The one thing that gets Zoltan out of his lab: a volunteer. **He pays adventurers to drink his experimental potions**, then watches, takes notes and asks very personal questions about how it feels.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0021"></div>


- The player always chooses whether to drink. Valor-33 makes sure of that.
- He pays **5 gp** per potion, or **10 gp** if they drink it without asking what's in it.
- **One test per character per long rest.** He needs time to write up his notes.

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 69}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Ampersand’s Apothecary

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-AMPERSAND"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0022"></div>


**Experimental Potion Results (d8).** Each lasts 1 hour unless stated.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0023"></div>


| d8 | Result |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | **Ghost Poo.** Your spirit floats out of your body and hovers above you. You can see but can't act. It snaps back after 1 minute. |
| 2 | **Giggles.** You laugh at everything. Disadvantage on Stealth, and on Charisma checks where laughing would be rude. |
| 3 | **Honesty.** You can't tell a deliberate lie. |
| 4 | **Invisibility Burps.** Each time you burp, you turn invisible until the start of your next turn. You burp every few minutes, whether you want to or not. |
| 5 | **Mimicry.** You can perfectly copy any voice you hear, but only in a whisper. |
| 6 | **Animal Tongue.** Every time you speak, it comes out as a random animal noise. You understand everyone else perfectly. |
| 7 | **Height Swap.** You and the nearest willing creature swap heights. Your gear does not adjust. |
| 8 | **It actually works!** Roll on the *Ampersand's Apothecaria* potion tables, or the GM picks a useful common potion. Zoltan is as surprised as you are. |

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0024"></div>


{{note
##### Using Ampersand's Apothecaria
Groups using the *Ampersand's Apothecaria* book can learn foraging and brewing from Zoltan. Use that book's rules. Without it, Zoltan is simply a shop and a source of jobs.
}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM REFERENCE}}

{{pageNumber 70}}

\page {sw-reorder}

{{swpilot-eyebrow SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD / GM GUIDE / CONTINUED}}

# Ampersand’s Apothecary

{{swpilot-flow

<div class="swanchor" id="COP-AMPERSAND"></div>



<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0025"></div>


### Jobs and Hooks

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0026"></div>


- **Ingredient runs:** he always needs something, usually from somewhere dangerous.
- **His Gathering job** on the Guild board wants balm fungi and mandrake root, and he pays for quality, not rubbish (Copper Chapter).
- **The monster bait** (Copper Chapter): bring him a sample from an attacked cart, and he names its rare ingredient, **hollowhart musk** worked into axle grease, and the one broker who imports it: **Penhallow**.
- **His cousin, Tobble,** keeps the sign-in ledger at the mines. Mention Zoltan and Tobble is far more helpful.
- **In the war** (Gold Chapter), Zoltan fights alongside the town with his alchemical cannon. Valor-33 carries the ammunition and complains.

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0027"></div>


### Rumours About Ampersand (d4)

<div class="swanchor" id="c06b0028"></div>


| d4 | Rumour |
|:--|:--|
| 1 | "His shop's exploded twice since he arrived. He rebuilt it both times by the next morning." |
| 2 | "He paid me ten silver to drink something green. I barked for an hour." |
| 3 | "He's not really called Ampersand, you know." |
| 4 | "Nobody's ever seen him serve a customer. The tin man does all that." |

}}
