{{swblock,#c21b0086

### The Rook's Nest and the Catacombs

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0087

Use the **Silver** tables. At this rank, treat every row as one step easier.

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0088

## The Platinum Pass

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0089

### Levels 9–10

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0090

Balanced for four **level 9** characters. Anywhere not listed here, use the **Gold** tables: at this rank they're easy or medium.

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0091

### The Feywoods

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0092

*Now the heart of the story. Rot creatures are in the Bestiary.*

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0093

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **2 rot hounds** and **4 rotlings** |
| Medium | **A rot beast** and **4 rotlings** |
| Hard | **A rot beast** and **3 rot hounds** |
| Deadly | **A rot behemoth** and **4 rotlings** |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0094

*Once both obelisks are healed or destroyed, the rot stops spreading. Roll only once a day here after that, and replace rot creatures with **manticores** and **chimeras**.*

}}

}}

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c21b0095

### The Southern Ruins (optional)

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0096

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **3 gargoyles** |
| Medium | **4 gargoyles** and **2 suits of animated armour** |
| Hard | **A shield guardian** and **2 suits of animated armour** |
| Deadly | **A stone golem** and **2 gargoyles**, guarding the airship |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0097

### The Labyrinth (optional)

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0098

Use the **room table** below, and the encounter tables for the party's rank.

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0099

## The Undercity and the Wild Lands

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0100

These areas are open at any rank. Use them as written for the rank shown. For a party a rank higher, treat each line as one step easier and add one more creature.

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0101

### The Undercity (Copper, levels 3–4)

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0102

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **4 giant rats** |
| Medium | **3 swarms of rats** and **4 giant rats** |
| Hard | **A wererat** and **2 giant rats** |
| Deadly | **2 wererats** and **2 swarms of rats** |

}}

}}

}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD | Encounter Tables}}

{{swblock,#c21b0103

### The High Shelves (Silver, levels 5–6)

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0104

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **3 hippogriffs** |
| Medium | **2 griffons** and **a hippogriff** |
| Hard | **4 griffons** |
| Deadly | **A wyvern** and **2 griffons**, in a storm |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0105

### The Frostfang Peaks (Gold, levels 7–8)

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0106

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **2 polar bears** |
| Medium | **2 winter wolves** |
| Hard | **A young white dragon** and **2 ice mephits** |
| Deadly | **A frost giant** and **2 winter wolves** |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0107

### The Cinderreach (Gold, levels 7–8)

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0108

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **A hell hound** and **2 magma mephits** |
| Medium | **2 hell hounds** and **an azer** |
| Hard | **A fire elemental** and **2 magma mephits** |
| Deadly | **A young red dragon** and **2 magma mephits** |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0109

*If the party carries **Vigilance**, she knows a red dragon when she sees one. Expect her to have opinions.*

}}

}}

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c21b0110

## The Mithril Pass

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0111

### Level 11+

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0112

Balanced for four **level 11** characters. The tower itself has **no wandering monsters**: everything inside it is in the Mithril Chapter. Anywhere else, use the **Platinum** tables, one step easier, except:

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0113

### The Feywoods and the Deep Labyrinth

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0114

| Difficulty | Encounter |
|:--|:--|
| Easy | **A rot beast** and **4 rotlings** |
| Medium | **A rot beast** and **3 rot hounds** |
| Hard | **A rot behemoth** and **4 rotlings** |
| Deadly | **2 rot behemoths** |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0115

## The Labyrinth: Room Table

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0116

Each floor has **d6 + 3 rooms**. Roll a **d12** for each.

}}

}}

}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD | Encounter Tables}}
{{pageNumber 370}}

\page {sw,sw-body}

{{sweyebrow 21 · Encounter Tables}}

{{swbody

{{swcolumns

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c21b0117

| d12 | Room |
|:--:|:--|
| 1 | **An empty corridor,** with a note or mark left by someone who came before. |
| 2 | **A trap.** DC and damage by rank: Copper DC 12, 2d10; Silver DC 13, 4d10; Gold DC 15, 6d10; Platinum DC 16, 8d10; Mithril DC 18, 10d10. |
| 3–6 | **A lair.** Roll on any area's table for the party's rank. |
| 7 | **A riddle door.** A wrong answer sets off a trap (as 2). |
| 8 | **A cache:** crafting materials, rare ingredients or coins. |
| 9 | **A shrine** to one of the eight gods. The party can take a short rest here safely. |
| 10 | **A mirror room.** A shadow copy of one random character steps out (see *The Shadow Copies*, Bestiary). |
| 11 | **An ooze garden:** **gelatinous cubes**, **ochre jellies** and **gray oozes**, scaled to the party's rank. |
| 12 | **The pedestal.** This floor is done. Touch it to unlock teleporting here next time. |

}}

{{swblock,#c21b0118

*The Labyrinth gives items and materials, not experience (see the Platinum Chapter).*

}}

}}

{{swcol



}}

}}

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0007

## Silver Chapter

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0008

{{monster,frame
## Goblin Rider
*Small humanoid (goblinoid), neutral evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 15 (leather armour, shield)
**Hit Points** :: 21 (6d6)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|8 (−1)|14 (+2)|10 (+0)|10 (+0)|10 (+0)|8 (−1)|
___
**Skills** :: Animal Handling +4, Stealth +6
**Senses** :: darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 10
**Languages** :: Common, Goblin
**Challenge** :: 1/2 (100 XP)
___
***Steering Hooks.*** The rider controls its ogre with hooks through the ogre's nose and arms. While the rider is mounted, the ogre has **advantage** on attacks against the creature the rider last goaded.

***Gone Wild.*** If the rider dies or is knocked off, the ogre goes wild. On each of its turns, it attacks the **nearest creature**, goblins included.

***Nimble Escape.*** The goblin can take the Disengage or Hide action as a bonus action.
### Actions
***Scimitar.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 5 (1d6 + 2) slashing damage.

***Shortbow.*** *Ranged Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, range 80/320 ft., one target. *Hit:* 5 (1d6 + 2) piercing damage.
### Bonus Actions
***Goad.*** The rider points the ogre at one creature it can see.
}}

}}

}}

}}

}}

{{monster,frame
## Goblin Hexer
*Small humanoid (goblinoid), neutral evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 13 (hide armour)
**Hit Points** :: 27 (6d6 + 6)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|8 (−1)|14 (+2)|12 (+1)|10 (+0)|10 (+0)|14 (+2)|
___
**Skills** :: Stealth +6
**Senses** :: darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 10
**Languages** :: Common, Goblin
**Challenge** :: 1 (200 XP)
___
***Nimble Escape.*** The goblin can take the Disengage or Hide action as a bonus action.

***Spellcasting.*** The hexer's spellcasting ability is Charisma (spell save DC 12, +4 to hit with spell attacks). It knows:

Cantrips (at will): *chill touch*, *minor illusion*
1st level (3 slots): ***bane***, *thunderwave*
2nd level (1 slot): *darkness*
### Actions
***Bone Knife.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 4 (1d4 + 2) piercing damage.
}}

}}

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c22b0010

{{monster,frame
## Kobold Trapmaster
*Small humanoid (kobold), lawful evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 14 (leather armour)
**Hit Points** :: 45 (10d6 + 10)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|8 (−1)|16 (+3)|12 (+1)|14 (+2)|12 (+1)|8 (−1)|
___
**Skills** :: Perception +3, Sleight of Hand +5, Stealth +5
**Senses** :: darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 13
**Languages** :: Common, Draconic
**Challenge** :: 2 (450 XP)
___
***Pack Tactics.*** The trapmaster has advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of its allies is within 5 feet of the creature and isn't incapacitated.

***Sunlight Sensitivity.*** While in sunlight, the trapmaster has disadvantage on attack rolls and Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.

***Home Ground.*** The trapmaster knows every trap in its warren and never triggers them.
### Actions
***Multiattack.*** The trapmaster makes two hand crossbow attacks.

***Hand Crossbow.*** *Ranged Weapon Attack:* +5 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. *Hit:* 6 (1d6 + 3) piercing damage.

***Oil Flask.*** *Ranged Weapon Attack:* +5 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. *Hit:* The target is soaked in oil. Until it spends an action wiping it off, it takes an extra 5 (2d4) fire damage whenever it takes fire damage.
### Bonus Actions
***Cut the Line.*** The trapmaster sets off one trap it can see within 60 feet.

***Light It.*** The trapmaster throws a torch onto an oil-soaked area within 30 feet, setting it alight.
}}

}}

}}

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0011

{{monster,frame
## Catacomb Abomination
*Huge undead, neutral evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 13 (natural armour)
**Hit Points** :: 172 (15d12 + 75)
**Speed** :: 20 ft., climb 20 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|20 (+5)|6 (−2)|20 (+5)|3 (−4)|8 (−1)|5 (−3)|
___
**Damage Resistances** :: necrotic
**Damage Immunities** :: poison
**Condition Immunities** :: exhaustion, frightened, poisoned, prone
**Senses** :: darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 9
**Languages** :: none, but it moans with dozens of voices
**Challenge** :: 7 (2,900 XP)
___
***Too Big for the Walkways.*** The abomination can't enter a space less than 10 feet wide, but it can reach into one.

***Many Hands.*** The abomination can grapple up to three creatures at once.

***Turn Resistance.*** The abomination has advantage on saving throws against any effect that turns undead.

***Pearl-Bound.*** When the abomination dies, the pearl's magic breaks. Every undead it raised within 1 mile collapses, still.
### Actions
***Multiattack.*** The abomination makes three Grasping Arm attacks, or two Grasping Arm attacks and one Crush.

***Grasping Arm.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +8 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. *Hit:* 14 (2d8 + 5) bludgeoning damage, and the target is grappled (escape DC 16) and pulled up to 10 feet towards the abomination.

***Crush.*** One creature grappled by the abomination makes a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, taking 27 (4d10 + 5) bludgeoning damage on a failure, or half as much on a success.
}}

}}

}}

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c22b0012

{{monster,frame
## Selene Blackwood
*Medium humanoid (tiefling), lawful evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 15 (studded leather)
**Hit Points** :: 85 (10d8 + 40)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|10 (+0)|18 (+4)|18 (+4)|13 (+1)|11 (+0)|20 (+5)|
___
**Saving Throws** :: Dex +7, Wis +3, Cha +8
**Skills** :: Deception +11, Insight +3, Persuasion +11, Stealth +7
**Damage Resistances** :: fire
**Senses** :: darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 10
**Languages** :: Common, Infernal, Thieves' Cant
**Challenge** :: 5 (1,800 XP)
___
***The Perfect Host.*** If Selene starts a fight from a friendly conversation, she has advantage on her first attack.

***Sneak Attack (1/Turn).*** Selene deals an extra 10 (3d6) damage when she hits with an attack and has advantage, or when another enemy of the target is within 5 feet of it.

***Innate Spellcasting.*** Selene's spellcasting ability is Charisma (spell save DC 16). She can cast *darkness* once per day.
### Actions
***Multiattack.*** Selene makes two Crackling Whip attacks, or two dagger attacks.

***Crackling Whip.*** *Melee Spell Attack:* +8 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. *Hit:* 12 (2d6 + 5) lightning damage, and the target can't take reactions until the start of its next turn.

***Dagger.*** *Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack:* +7 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. *Hit:* 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage.
### Bonus Actions
***Cunning Action.*** Selene takes the Dash, Disengage or Hide action.
### Reactions
***Hellish Rebuke (1/Day).*** When a creature Selene can see damages her, it makes a DC 16 Dexterity saving throw, taking 16 (3d10) fire damage on a failure, or half as much on a success.
}}

}}

}}

}}

## Blackwood Servant
*Medium humanoid (any race), any alignment*
___
**Armor Class** :: 10
**Hit Points** :: 1 (minion)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|14 (+2)|10 (+0)|10 (+0)|10 (+0)|10 (+0)|8 (−1)|
___
**Skills** :: Athletics +4
**Senses** :: passive Perception 10
**Languages** :: understands Common but can't speak
**Challenge** :: 0 (0 XP)
___
***Minion.*** Any damage knocks the servant out of the fight: it drops, or cowers in a corner.

***Terrified.*** The servant never attacks to harm. It obeys only out of fear.
### Actions
***Seize.*** The servant grapples one creature within 5 feet (escape DC 12).

{{swblock,#c22b0014

## Gold Chapter

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0015

### The Greenhart Plants

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0016

All Greenhart plants share these traits:
- **Damage Vulnerabilities:** fire.
- **Condition Immunities:** blinded, deafened, exhaustion.
- **Senses:** blindsight 30 ft. (blind beyond), passive Perception 10.
- **Languages:** none.

}}

}}

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c22b0017

{{monster,frame
## Greenhart Drone
*Small plant, unaligned*
___
**Armor Class** :: 12
**Hit Points** :: 22 (5d6 + 5)
**Speed** :: 20 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|12 (+1)|14 (+2)|12 (+1)|2 (−4)|10 (+0)|3 (−4)|
___
**Challenge** :: 1/2 (100 XP)
___
***Docile.*** If the mother plant that spawned it dies, the drone stops fighting entirely. It won't even defend itself.

***Variety.*** Roll a d6 or choose. The drone gains that trait:

**1. Bloodvine Tendril.** A creature hit by its Lash is grappled (escape DC 12).
**2. Bloodthorn Vine.** Its Lash deals an extra 3 (1d6) piercing damage.
**3. Lurking Vine.** While it stays still, it looks like an ordinary plant. It has advantage on its first attack.
**4. Psychedelic Shroom.** Instead of a Lash, it puffs spores: each creature within 10 feet makes a DC 12 Wisdom save or has disadvantage on attacks until the end of its next turn, as the world swims.
**5. Explosive Cactus.** When it dies, each creature within 10 feet makes a DC 12 Dexterity save, taking 7 (2d6) piercing damage on a failure, or half as much on a success.
**6. Shrinking Plant.** A creature hit by its Lash makes a DC 12 Constitution save or shrinks to half size until the end of its next turn (as the *reduce* effect of *enlarge/reduce*).
### Actions
***Lash.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +4 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. *Hit:* 6 (1d8 + 2) piercing damage.
}}

}}

}}

}}

}}

___
**Armor Class** :: 12 (natural armour)
**Hit Points** :: 85 (10d10 + 30)

___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|

***Sweet Scent.*** A creature that starts its turn within 30 feet of the plant makes a DC 13 Wisdom saving throw. On a failure, it must use its movement this turn to move towards the plant.
### Actions

A swallowed creature is blinded and restrained, has total cover and takes 10 (3d6) acid damage at the start of each of its turns. The plant can hold one creature at a time.

### The Blackwood Army

{{swblock,#c22b0024

{{monster,frame
## Azazel Blackwood
*Medium humanoid (tiefling), lawful evil*
___
**Armor Class** :: 18 (infernal plate)
**Hit Points** :: 152 (16d8 + 80)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|

***Greatsword.*** *Melee Weapon Attack:* +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. *Hit:* 12 (2d6 + 5) slashing damage plus 7 (2d6) fire damage.

Each creature within 10 feet makes a DC 16 Constitution saving throw, taking 22 (5d8) necrotic damage on a failure, or half as much on a success. Azazel regains hit points equal to half the total necrotic damage dealt. **The more creatures crowd him, the more he heals.**

## Platinum Chapter

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0036

### Rot Creatures

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0037

The rot twists the Feywoods' animals into swollen, splitting things. All rot creatures share this trait:

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0038

> ***Death Burst.*** When the creature dies, each creature within 5 feet (10 feet for the behemoth) makes a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, it takes the listed necrotic damage, and **its hit point maximum is reduced by the same amount.**
>
> A long rest doesn't restore it. Only *greater restoration* does. A hit point maximum can't fall below **half** its normal value this way.

}}

> ***Rotworms.*** An infested creature takes 7 (2d6) necrotic damage at the start of each of its turns. Any creature can use an action to burn the worms out with even a small flame (a torch, a fire cantrip), which ends the infestation. *Lesser restoration* also ends it. If the worms aren't removed within **3 rounds**, they reach the heart: the creature's hit point maximum is reduced by 10 (as Death Burst), and the worms die.

{{swblock,#c22b0045

### The Shadow Copies

}}

{{swblock,#c22b0046

Lirien's third trial (Platinum Chapter). There's no stat block: **each copy uses its character's own sheet.**

}}

}}

}}

}}

{{footnote SHADOW OVER SILVERWOOD | Bestiary}}
{{pageNumber 387}}

\page {sw,sw-body}

{{sweyebrow 22 · Bestiary}}

{{swbody

{{swcolumns

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c22b0047

- **Same everything:** level, hit points, AC from armour and Dexterity, ability scores, skills, class features, spells and spell slots.
- **Ordinary gear:** no magic items. Magic weapons become ordinary ones; magic armour gives only its normal AC; items with charges don't work.
- **No consumables:** copies don't drink potions or read scrolls.
- **Shadow-made:** at 0 hit points a copy fades away. It doesn't make death saves.
- **Tactics:** the copies fight as a real team. They focus fire, protect their healer and use the party's own favourite tricks against them.
- **Experience:** treat each copy as a creature of a CR equal to its character's level.
- **If it goes badly,** Lirien gets bored and waves it away.

}}

}}

{{swcol

{{swblock,#c22b0048

***Sticky Body.*** When a creature **misses** the spider with a melee weapon attack, the weapon sticks fast inside it. Pulling it free takes an action and a DC 12 Strength check.

### Actions

***Regurgitate (Recharge 5–6).*** The spider spits a 30-foot cone of stone, bones, skulls and old weapons. Each creature there makes a DC 17 Dexterity saving throw, taking 44 (8d10) bludgeoning damage on a failure, or half as much on a success.

{{monster,frame,wide
## Vartius Xanthos
*Medium humanoid (human), lawful neutral*
___
**Armor Class** :: 17 (robe of the archmagi)
**Hit Points** :: 247 (33d8 + 99)
**Speed** :: 30 ft.
___
|STR|DEX|CON|INT|WIS|CHA|
|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|
|10 (+0)|14 (+2)|16 (+3)|22 (+6)|16 (+3)|18 (+4)|
___
**Saving Throws** :: Con +9, Int +12, Wis +9
**Skills** :: Arcana +18, History +12, Insight +9
**Condition Immunities** :: charmed, frightened
**Senses** :: truesight 30 ft., passive Perception 13
**Languages** :: Common, Draconic, Elvish, Infernal, Sylvan and many more
**Challenge** :: 15 (13,000 XP)
___
***Legendary Resistance (3/Day).*** If Vartius fails a saving throw, he can choose to succeed instead.

***Robe of the Archmagi.*** Vartius has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

***Spellcasting.*** Vartius's spellcasting ability is Intelligence (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks). He can cast:

At will: *mage hand*, *minor illusion*
3/day each: ***counterspell***, ***shield***, *misty step*
2/day each: *dimension door*, *fireball* (cast at 6th level, 10d6)
1/day each: *chain lightning*, *cone of cold*, *globe of invulnerability*, *wall of force*
### Actions
***Multiattack.*** Vartius makes two Arcane Bolt attacks, or casts a spell and makes one Arcane Bolt attack.

***Arcane Bolt.*** *Ranged Spell Attack:* +12 to hit, range 120 ft., one target. *Hit:* 22 (4d10) force damage.

***Soul Wrench (Recharge 5–6).*** One creature Vartius can see within 60 feet makes a DC 20 Charisma saving throw, taking 44 (8d10) necrotic damage on a failure, or half as much on a success. Vartius regains hit points equal to the damage dealt.
### Legendary Actions
Vartius can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one option can be used at a time, and only at the end of another creature's turn. He regains spent legendary actions at the start of his turn.

***Bolt.*** Vartius makes one Arcane Bolt attack.

***Blink.*** Vartius teleports up to 30 feet to a space he can see.

***Unmake (Costs 2 Actions).*** Each creature within 15 feet of Vartius makes a DC 20 Strength saving throw. On a failure, it takes 16 (3d10) force damage, is pushed 20 feet away and is knocked prone.
### Lair Actions: the Soul Machine
On initiative count 20 (losing ties), the soul machine acts. Choose one; don't use the same one twice in a row:
- **Soul Flicker.** One creature makes a DC 15 Charisma save or swaps places with another creature of Vartius's choice within 30 feet.
- **Grinding Gears.** A 20-foot square of floor lurches. Each creature there makes a DC 15 Dexterity save, taking 22 (4d10) bludgeoning damage on a failure, or half as much on a success.
- **The Machine Hums.** Vartius regains 20 hit points.
### Ending A: Unbound
When the players tell him they freed Lysander, Vartius stops defending himself and fights to destroy. From then on, **Soul Wrench recharges on a 4–6**, but he **no longer casts *counterspell* or *shield***. Telling him the truth makes him deadlier and easier to hit.
}}

# Handouts

## The Handout Pack

Use the separate Handout Pack and current Evidence pack. The evidence index identifies the branch-specific sheets; the unfinished crypt puzzle props still use their written no-props option. The words needed to run each scene remain in the chapters.

{{note
##### Running the job board
Each chapter opens with a picture of its job board. Turn the book round and let the players look, or read the posters aloud and play it in the theatre of the mind. If you've printed the pack, pin up the posters for the party's current pass.
